LEASE ADDENDUM- DOG HAUS
ADDENDUM TO LEASE AGREEMENT
THIS ADDENDUM TO LEASE AGREEMENT (this “Addendum”) is effective as of
2017 (the “fifective Date”). and is being signed simultaneously with the Lease
(the
“Leas&’)
dated
2017 between
_________________________
(the “Franchisee” or “Tenant”) and
(the ‘Landlord”) for the real property
commonly known as
___________________________
(the “Premises”).
1.
Incorporation and Precedence. This Addendum is incorporated into the Lease and
supersedes any conflicting provisions in it. Capitalized terms not otherwise defined in this
Addendum have the meanings as defined in the Lease.
2.
Background. The Tenant will operate a Dog Hans restaurant at the Premises
under a Franchise Agreement (the ‘Franchise Agreement”) with Dog Hans International, LLC
(the “Franchisor’). By entering into a franchise relationship with the Franchisor. the Tenant has
agreed to grant the Franchisor a security interest in the Lease, all of the furniture, fixtures.
inventory and supplies located in the Premises as collateral for the payment of any obligation.
liability or other amount owed by the Tenant or its affiliates to the Franchisor under the
Franchise Agreement. The Franchise Agreement also requires that the Lease contain certain
provisions that the Tenant is requesting the Landlord to include.
3
Marks. The Tenant has the right to display the trade and service marks of the
Franchisor in accordance with the specifications required by the Franchisor. subject only to the
provisions of applicable law. for the terni of the Lease.
4.
Easement. The Landlord grants to the Tenant during the term of the Lease a non-
exclusive right and easement over that portion of the property as may be required by the Tenant
to improve. renovate. repair. replace and maintain the Premises or replace its signage or its panel
on the pylon sign for the property. The Tenant has the right to chance or alter the signage at any
time during the term of the Lease provided the signage is in compliance with all applicable
governmental codes and regulations. The signage may include: (a) signaae on the exterior front
wall of the Premises: (b) signage on another exterior portion of the Premises: (c) a separate pylon
sign on the property: (d) separate signage on the property. (e) a panel on the pylon sign for the
property: and (t) other signage which may be required by the Franchisor or agreed upon by the
Landlord and the Tenant.
5.
Copies of Reports. The Landlord agrees to provide copies of all revenue and
other infonnation and data in Landlord’s possession related to the operation of the Tenant’s Dog
Haus restaurant on a timely basis as the Franchisor may request. during the term ofthe Lease.
6.
Notice of Default. The Landlord will give written notice to the Franchisor
(concurrently with the giving of such notice to the Tenant) of any defaults (a ‘l)efault”) by the
Tenant under the Lease by certified mail. return receipt requested. or by nationally recognized
overnight courier service, at the following address or to such other address as the Franchisor may
provide to Landlord from time to time:
1S379180.5/043245.0001

DocuSign Envelope ID: E708E679-CDAF4576-B681-50092EF8D6F8
Dog Hans International. LLC
22 Central Court
Pasadena, California 91105
Attention: Franchise Administrator
Such notice u-ill grant the Franchisor the right, but not the obligation, to cure any Default. if the
Tenant fails to do so. within an additional fifteen (15) days after the expiration of the period in
which the Tenant may cure the Default under the Lease.
7.
Franchisor’s Assumption of Lease. In the event of a default of the Lease by
Tenant or the default of the Franchise Agreement by Tenant, and upon written notice by the
Franchisor to have the Lease assigned to the Franchisor or a qualified franchisee of Franchisot- as
lessee (the “Assignment Notice”), (i) the Franchisor or a qualified franchisee of Franchisor u-ill
become the lessee of the Premises and u-ill be liable for all obligations under the Lease arising
after the date of the Assignment Notice and (ii) the Landlord will recognize the Franchisor or a
qualified franchisee ofFranchisor as the lessee of the Prenuses effective as of the date of the
Assignment Notice. Notwithstanding anything in the Lease to the contrary. Landlord agrees that
the lease and the rght. title and interest of Tenant and any subsequent or successor Tenant
thereunder. may be assigned to Franchisor or its parents. subsidiaries or affiliates or to a
qualified franchisee ofFrauchisor. In the event of an assignment to a Franchisor entity.
Franchisor shall at all times have the right to reassign the Lease. without charge and without
Landlord consent being required to a qualified franchisee of Franchisor and the Franchisor entity
shall thereupon be released from any further liability under the Lease.
8.
Default Under Franchise Agreement Any Default under the Lease which is not
cured by Tenant within any applicable cure period also constitutes grounds for termination of the
Franchise Agreement.
9.
Use and Competing Business. Tenant shall only use the Premises for the purpose
of operating a “Dog Haus” restaurant that engages in the sale of hot dogs. sausages. burgers and
other related food products and side dishes for dine-in or take-out. including any incidental
merchandising as is customary in a Dog Hans restaurant now or in the future. Landlord shall not
during the term of the Lease. including any renewals or extensions, permit directly or indirectly.
another restaurant in the shopping center with more than 6 beers on tap or that sells hot dogs.
sausages. or burgers for dine-in or take-out. In the event that Landlord breaches this provision.
then Tenant shall be entitled to an immediate reduction of its rent to One Dollar ($1.00) per month
until such time as the breach is cured if possible or for the remainder of the term of the Lease and
any renewals or extensions thereof if the breach cannot be cured.
10.
Non-Disturbance. So long as the Lease term continues and the Tenant is not in
Default tinder the Lease. the Tenant’s use, possession and enjoyment of the Premises will not be
interfered with by any lender of the Landlord or any other person. The Landlord agrees to use its
best efforts to obtain prior to commencement of the Lease any documents necessary to ensure the
foregoing. including a Subordination. Non-Disturbance and Attornment Agreement or similar
agreement.
18379180.5/043245.0001

11.
Latent Defects. Notwithstanding anything contained in the Lease. Landlord will
repair any latent defects to the Premises discovered by Tenant and communicate in nting to
Landlord within one (1) year of execution of the Lease.
12.
Amendment. The Landlord and the Tenant will not cancel. tenninate. modify or
amend the Lease including, without limitation, the Franchisors rights under this Addendum.
without the Franchiso?s prior written consent.
13.
Benefits and Successors. The benefits of this Addendum inure to the Franchisor
and to its successor and assigns.
14.
Remaining Provisions Unaffected. Those parts of the Lease that are not expressly
modified by this Addendum remain in full force and effect.

effective on the Effective Date, regardless of the actual date of sinature.

TO:
Honorable Patrick Collins, Mayor of the City of Cheyenne
Honorable Pete Layboum, City Council Member, Ward I
Honorable Scott Roybal, City Council Member, Ward I
Honorable Jeff White, City Council Member, Ward I
Honorable Bryan M. Cook, City Council Member, Ward 2
Honorable Dr. Mark Rinne, City Council Member, Ward 2
Honorable Tom Segrave, City Council Member, Ward 2
Honorable Dr. Michelle Aldrich, City Council Member, Ward3
Honorable Richard Johnson, City Council Member, Ward 3
Honorable Ken Esquibel, City Council Member, Ward 3
Dear Mr. Mayor and City Council Members,
We are writing to request your consideration for a City of Cheyenne Bar and Grill Liquor License
for our establishment, the Dog Haus Biergarten. Our local team recently secured the franchise
after the previous owner abandoned the franchise and closed the doors. We are excited and
looking forward to reopening this unique dining experience to the community.
The Dog Haus Biergarten is an important addition to our community, as it provides a
one-of-a-kind dining experience that is not currently available in the area. With our team’s
passion for the community and our commitment to being involved in local events and
fundraising initiatives, we believe that the Dog Haus Biergarten can become an integral part of
the Cheyenne community.
By obtaining a Bar and Grill Liquor License, we will be able to better serve our customers and
enhance their dining experience. We understand the responsibility that comes with selling
alcohol, and we are committed to conducting all alcohol sales in a responsible manner and in
compliance with all state laws and regulations.
We understand that the City of Cheyenne takes the granting of liquor licenses very seriously,
and we want to assure you that we are committed to operating the Dog Haus Biergarten in a
safe and responsible manner. We will ensure that all employees are properly trained in the

Mr. Mayor and City Council Members
Page 2
April 3, 2023
sale and service of alcohol, and we will maintain strict adherence to all state laws and
regulations governing the sale of alcohol.
We believe that the Dog Haus Biergarten can be a valuable contributor to the Cheyenne
community, and we are committed to making a positive impact by being involved in local events
and fundraising initiatives. We appreciate your consideration of our request for a City of
Cheyenne Bar and Grill Liquor License.
Thank you for your time and attention.
Sincerely,
Clifford Johnson, Owner
Marlena Schu tz, 9ie)
Morgan Amberson, Owner
Charles Schultz, Owner

WE GET CRAFTY
When eating a dog, sausage or burger, you can work up a mighty thirst. Fortu
nately, Dog Haus has the answer: the best craft beers around. We are passio
nate about curating tap lists that high light local brews, featuring something for
everyone and emphasizing both quality and variety. Its important to us that th
e beer we serve is as memorable as our food, and that each Dog Haus location
‘s tap list is as unique as its community.
CRAFT BEER

JACKSON STREET AND DOUGLAS STREET." 
 
BE IT ORDAINED BY THE GOVERNING BODY OF THE CITY OF 
CHEYENNE, WYOMING: 
 
Section 1.  That the City of Cheyenne hereby finds as follows: 
 
(a) 
That Christian and Missionary Alliance Church of Cheyenne, owners of:  
A portion of Tract 12, Dell Range Addition, Laramie County, Wyoming, more 
particularly described as follows:  
Tract 12 Less Alliance Addition, 1st & 2nd Filings as described BK 1148 PG 1486; 
also less (.85 ac) desc as beg at the northeast corner of Tract 12, the S 00 deg 03' 
56" W, along the east line of Tract 12, 120.00'; the N 89 deg 25' 35" W, 309.91' to 
the southeast corner or Alliance Addition, 2nd Filing; the N 00 deg 00' 31" E, 
along the east line of sd Alliance Addition, 2nd Filing, 120.00' to the north line of 
sd Tract 12; the S 89 deg 25' 35" E, along sd north line, 310.02' to the point of 
beg, Dell Range Addition 
have petitioned the governing body of the City of Cheyenne to annex the real 
property described herein and to make it a part thereof; 
(b) 
That an accurate annexation map of said land is attached to this ordinance as 
Exhibit A, said map prepared by Gary N. Grigsby, a Professional Land Surveyor, 
licensed in the State of Wyoming, PLS Number 9283; 
(c) 
That the annexation of said area is for the protection of health, safety, and welfare 
of the persons residing in the area and the City of Cheyenne; 
(d) 
That the urban development of the area sought to be annexed will constitute a 
natural, geographical, economical and social part of the City of Cheyenne; 
(e) 
That the area is a logical and feasible addition to the City and that the extension of 
basic services such as water and sewer systems, police and fire protection and 
other services customarily available in the City of Cheyenne can, within reason, 
be furnished; 
(f) 
That the area is adjacent to the City of Cheyenne and is currently zoned MR 
Medium-density Residential by the Laramie County Commissioners; and, 
(g) 
That the City of Cheyenne does not own or operate its own electrical utility, and 
its governing body is prepared to designate a utility to serve the annexed area 
pursuant to W.S. 15-1-410, and to authorize the designated utility to serve the 
2:10 pm, Apr 04 2023

entire annexed area. 
 
Section 2.  That all of the real property described in Exhibit A is hereby annexed to the 
City of Cheyenne, Wyoming, and shall become a part thereof.  
 
Section 3.  That the City Clerk is directed to file an executed copy of this annexation 
ordinance and map (Exhibit A) with the County Clerk and Ex-Officio Register of Deeds for 
Laramie County, Wyoming, and shall also notify the City Engineer's Office to add said area to 
the official City Map of the City of Cheyenne, Wyoming. 
 
Section 4.  Sections, paragraphs, sentences, clauses and phrases of this ordinance are 
severable. If any phrase, clause, sentence, paragraph or section of this ordinance is declared 
illegal or unconstitutional, such illegality or unconstitutionality shall not affect any of the 
remaining phrases, clauses, sentences, paragraphs and sections.  
 
Section 5.  That this ordinance shall be in full force and effect upon its publication.

CITY OF CHEYENNE GOVERNING BODY 
MARCH 27, 2023 
STAFF REPORT 
 
FILE: 
UDC-23-00065:  5224 Ridge Road - Annexation 
REQUEST: 
An annexation of 1.29 acres of land. 
LOCATION: 
West of Ridge Road, between Jackson Street and Douglas Street 
APPLICANT: 
Clayton Howell 
AGENT: 
John Lopeman 
OWNERS: 
Jason Taylor – Cheyenne Alliance Church 
PURPOSE: 
Owner request 
PREPARED BY: 
Erin Fagan, Planner II 
 
 
APPLICABLE CITY CODE SECTION(S) AND PLANS: 
 
• 
UDC 2.4.2 Annexations 
• 
PlanCheyenne 
  
  
  
  
  
  
  
  Planning and Development Department 
2101 O’Neil Avenue, Suite 202, Cheyenne WY 82001 
(Phone) 307-637-6282 
(Fax) 307-637-6366 

UDC-23-00065 
 
 
March 27, 2023 
 
BACKGROUND: 
 
The applicant is requesting annexing 1.29 acres of land. The land is currently developed with church building. 
A zone change is proposed concurrently with this annexation. All applications are anticipated to come to final 
consideration before the Governing Body at the same meeting.  
 
Land annexed to the City requires a City zone. The petition to annex has been accompanied by a submission 
to change the zoning from County MR to City MR. 
 
SURROUNDING LAND USE AND ZONING: 
 
 
The property is currently zoned MR in the County. The property is concurrently proposed to be annexed 
and rezoned to the City zone of MR. The City MR zone is intended for Medium-density Residential 
Development. 
 
ANALYSIS OF APPLICATION CONFORMANCE WITH REVIEW CRITERIA: 
 
To accept the petition to annex and approve the annexation Ordinance, the Governing Body must make 
the following findings required in WS 15-1-402; a detailed analysis of which is below: 
 
1) An annexation of the area is for the protection of the health, safety and welfare of the 
persons residing in the area and in the city or town.  [WS 15-1-402 (a) (i)] 
 
The annexation of this property will increase the health, safety, and welfare of the future 
occupants. By being in City limits, the property will receive access to City Fire and Police and 
sanitation services.  
 
The application complies with this review criterion. 
 
2) The urban development of the area sought to be annexed would constitute a natural, 
geographical, economical and social part of the annexing city or town. [WS 15-1-402 (a) (ii)] 
 
This property will constitute a natural, geographic, economic, and social part of the city. The 
property is a County pocket and adjacent to the City on all sides of the property. The contiguity 
to the City and the proximity to surrounding urban-style development makes the parcel a

UDC-23-00065 
 
 
March 27, 2023 
natural geographic part of the City. The development of this property will benefit those who live 
within the City and will be a part of the social and economic fabric that is Cheyenne.   
 
The application complies with this review criterion. 
   
3) The area sought to be annexed is a logical and feasible addition to the annexing city or town 
and the extension of basic and other services customarily available to residents of the city or 
town shall, within reason, be available to the area proposed to be annexed. [WS 15-1-402 (a) 
(iii)] 
 
The area is a logical and feasible addition to the City due to the reasons previously listed. All City 
services normally available to residents will also be made available. Police and fire protection 
will be better for the area upon annexation. Utilities exist in the area. 
 
The application complies with this review criterion. 
 
4) The area sought to be annexed is contiguous with or adjacent to the annexing city or town, or 
the area meets the requirements of W.S. 15-1-407. [WS 15-1-402 (a) (iv)] 
 
The area proposed for annexation is 100% contiguous.   
 
The application complies with this review criterion. 
 
5) If the city or town does not own or operate its own electric utility, its governing body is 
prepared to issue one (1) or more franchises as necessary to serve the annexed area pursuant 
to W.S. 15-1-410. [WS 15-1-402 (a) (v)] 
 
Utility franchises are in place to serve the annexed area.   
 
The application complies with this review criterion. 
    
6) The annexing city or town, not less than twenty (20) business days prior to the public hearing 
required by W.S. 15-1-405(a), has sent by certified mail to all landowners and affected public 
utilities within the territory a summary of the proposed annexation report as required under 
subsection (c) of this section and notice of the time, date and location of the public hearing 
required by W.S. 15-1-405(a). [WS 15-1-402 (a) (vi)] 
 
A certified mailing was made to the landowners and public utilities on March 13, 2023. The 
public hearing for this annexation is scheduled for March 27, 2023. Twenty (20) business days 
(on the City’s calendar) prior to the announced public hearing is February 27, 2023.  
 
The application complies with this review criterion.   
 
CONFORMANCE WITH APPLICABLE PLANS: 
 
The project is located in an area covered by the following plans: 
• 
Plan Cheyenne 
 
Policies within PlanCheyenne encourage the annexation of County pockets. This is a County Pocket.    
 

UDC-23-00065 
 
 
March 27, 2023 
The Future Land Use Map of PlanCheyenne, as updated by Connect 2045, anticipates this area of the 
community to develop with urban residential uses. These areas can develop with or without access to 
urban services. The area is within the Urban Service Boundary. 
 
STAFF REVIEW: 
 
City Engineering: 
 
1. Boundary data and legal descriptions appear in order. 
2. Double check survey ties to City Control Stations. 
3. Provide Coordinates for the NE and SE corners tied to City Control Stations. 
4. On the map (shaded parcel being annexed) provide the acreage figure. 
 
Environmental Health Department: 
Laramie County Small Wastewater System Regulations: 
The existing septic system shall be 
properly abandoned and inspected by this office. Contact Environmental Health (307) 633-4090. 
 
CONCLUSION: 
 
Staff is satisfied that the application meets the review criteria and therefore recommends approval of 
the annexation.   
 
The proposed annexation is a natural extension of the City, is contiguous to the current City limits, and 
services typically available to urban areas are available to this property.  
 
STAFF RECOMMENDATION: 
 
Staff recommends approval of the annexation of the property. 
 
ATTACHMENTS: 
1. Current Zoning Map 
2. Future Land Use Map 
3. Submitted Application 
4. Applicant-submitted annexation map
